## Placement

- `Core`: required to understand the topic or write a complete core answer.
- `Optional Advanced`: refinement, debate, criticism or enrichment that is safely skippable.
- `Practice`: PYQ, MCQ, model-answer or remediation improvement.
- `Flow only`: source content is sufficient but one or both master flows omit or distort it.

Every proposal must explain why the change affects learning or paper performance. Avoid rewriting
already sufficient Markdown merely to match a preferred style.
